TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于架构感知的解释审计协议，核心假设是解释方法的忠实度与模型原生决策机制的结构距离负相关。在WM-811K晶圆图数据集上，ViT-Tiny + Attention Rollout尽管分类精度较低，但忠实度显著优于Swin-Tiny/ResNet18+CBAM/DenseNet121+Grad-CAM；模型无关方法RISE超越所有原生方法，表明原生读取是兼容性原则而非最优性保证；不同扰动基准会反转忠实度排序，验证了忠实度是(模型、解释器、扰动算子)三元组的联合属性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业视觉检测系统依赖深度学习分类器，但其热力图解释可能视觉上合理却未能真正反映模型决策依据，现有解释方法缺乏对模型架构原生决策机制的结构性对齐审计。

Method: 提出架构感知解释审计协议：基于原生读取假设，以解释方法与模型原生决策机制的结构距离作为忠实度界标；采用三种子零填充扰动协议，使用Deletion AUC等指标量化忠实度；通过Swin-Tiny解耦架构族与读取结构、RISE模型无关控制、模糊填充敏感性分析、MVTec AD边界条件研究等方法验证协议有效性。

Result: 1) ViT-Tiny+Attention Rollout的Deletion AUC为0.211，显著优于Swin-Tiny/ResNet18+CBAM/DenseNet121+Grad-CAM（0.432-0.525），效应量|Cohen's d|>1.1；2) RISE将各架构族的Deletion AUC压缩至约0.1，超越所有原生方法；3) 模糊填充实验反转了家族排序，验证忠实度为三元组联合属性；4) MVTec AD上的边界条件研究显示结果依赖数据集/任务。

Conclusion: 解释路径应与模型架构基于读取结构进行协同设计，部署的热力图应附带定量忠实度指标；原生读取是兼容性原则而非最优性保证，忠实度排名是(模型、解释器、扰动算子)三元组的联合属性。

Abstract: Industrial visual inspection systems increasingly rely on deep classifiers whose heatmap explanations may appear visually plausible while failing to identify the image regions that actually drive model decisions. This paper operationalizes an architecture-aware explanation audit protocol grounded in the native-readout hypothesis: the perturbation-based faithfulness of an explanation method is bounded by its structural distance from the model's native decision mechanism. On WM-811K wafer maps (9 classes, 172k images) under a three-seed zero-fill perturbation protocol, ViT-Tiny + Attention Rollout attains Deletion AUC 0.211 against 0.432-0.525 for Swin-Tiny / ResNet18+CBAM / DenseNet121 + Grad-CAM (abs(Cohen's d) > 1.1), despite lower classification accuracy. Swin-Tiny disentangles architecture family from readout structure: despite being a Transformer, its spatial feature-map hierarchy makes it Grad-CAM compatible, showing that the operative factor is readout structure rather than architecture family. A model-agnostic control (RISE) compresses all families to Deletion AUC about 0.1, indicating the gap arises from the explainer pathway; notably, RISE outperforms all native methods, so native readout is a compatibility principle rather than an optimality guarantee. A blur-fill sensitivity analysis shows that the family ordering reverses under a different perturbation baseline, reinforcing that faithfulness rankings are joint properties of (model, explainer, perturbation operator) triples. An exploratory boundary-condition study on MVTec AD (pretrained models) indicates that audit results are dataset/task dependent and identifies conditions requiring qualification. The protocol yields actionable guidance: explanation pathways should be co-designed with model architectures based on readout structure, and deployed heatmaps should be accompanied by quantitative faithfulness metrics.

</details>


### [39] [Smooth Multi-Policy Causal Effect Estimation in Longitudinal Settings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14284)
*Wenxin Chen,Weishen Pan,Kyra Gan,Fei Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出PEQ-Net，通过策略感知重参数化实现多个动态治疗策略的联合估计，解决了传统独立估计方法存在的二阶偏差和有限样本方差膨胀问题。该方法利用共享策略编码器和核均值嵌入，在LTMLE矫正后稳定了有限样本方差，在半合成数据集上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的纵向因果推断方法对每个动态治疗策略独立估计，导致反事实间无法共享信息，产生结构性的未受控二阶偏差，即使经过标准LTMLE去偏后仍会放大有限样本方差。

Method: 提出策略感知的迭代条件期望(ICE) Q函数重参数化，通过共享表示实现联合估计；设计PEQ-Net架构，核心是共享策略编码器；使用核均值嵌入训练编码器，使表示空间反映总体层面的策略差异性；最后应用LTMLE矫正步骤，对二阶余项施加结构约束以稳定有限样本方差。

Result: 在半合成数据集上的实验表明，PEQ-Net一致优于现有的ICE-based方法，在评估密切相关的策略时尤其显著，实现了均方根误差的大幅降低。

Conclusion: 策略感知的联合估计框架能有效克服独立估计带来的二阶偏差问题，通过共享表征和结构约束提升多个动态治疗策略比较的统计效率和准确性。

Abstract: Comparative evaluation of multiple dynamic treatment policies is essential for healthcare and policy decisions, yet conventional longitudinal causal inference methods estimate each in isolation, preventing information sharing across counterfactuals. We demonstrate that this separate estimation paradigm induces a structurally uncontrolled second-order bias, inflating finite-sample variance even after standard debiasing with longitudinal targeted maximum likelihood estimation(LTMLE). To address this, we propose a policy-aware reparameterization of Iterative Conditional Expectation (ICE) Q-functions that enables joint estimation through shared representations. We implement this approach in the Policy-Encoded Q Network (PEQ-Net), an architecture centered on a shared policy encoder. The encoder is trained using kernel mean embeddings, ensuring that the learned representation space reflects population-level policy dissimilarities. After applying an LTMLE correction step, we prove this design imposes a structural constraint on the second-order remainder, thereby stabilizing finite-sample variance. Experiments on semi-synthetic datasets demonstrate that PEQ-Net consistently outperforms existing ICE-based methods, achieving substantial reductions in root-mean-square error, particularly when evaluating closely related policies.

</details>


### [40] [Minimal-Intervention KV Retention: A Design-Space Study and a Diversity-Penalty Survivor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14292)
*Libo Sun,Po-wei Harn,Peixiong He,Xiao Qin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在数学推理任务的小预算KV缓存压缩中，7种主流方法均被拒绝，而本文提出的α方法（通过贪心设施选址选择替代argmax-top-k）在预注册协议下显著优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 小预算KV缓存压缩空间拥挤且方法众多，但缺乏在受控条件下的系统性比较和严格的统计证据标准。

Method: 提出α方法，将TriAttention的argmax-top-k评分替换为基于贪心设施选址的选择，并引入V空间冗余惩罚（权重λ）。采用预注册协议，在冻结开发集上调优λ，在分离的留出集上验证。

Result: λ=0.5时，α在四个（模型，预算）单元格中的两个（Qwen b=128和Llama b=64）通过Bonferroni校正，无单元格显著为负，预注册Branch A触发。

Conclusion: 最小化的评分修改在受控条件下优于更重的结构重新设计，匹配内存、sympy评分和留出确认协议是揭示这种不对称性的关键证据标准。

Abstract: KV-cache compression at small budgets is a crowded design space spanning cache representation, head-wise routing, compression cadence, decoding behavior, and within-budget scoring. We study seven mechanisms across these five families under matched mean cache on long-form mathematical reasoning (MATH-500~\cite{hendrycks2021math}) with two distilled-reasoning models (Qwen-7B and Llama-8B variants of DeepSeek-R1-Distill~\cite{deepseek2025r1}) at budgets $b \in \{64, 128\}$. All seven were rejected. We then propose $α$, a one-function modification to the TriAttention~\cite{mao2026triattention} retention scorer that replaces argmax-top-$k$ with greedy facility-location-inspired selection under a V-space redundancy penalty controlled by a single weight $λ$. A pre-registered protocol tunes $λ$ on a frozen development split and confirms on a disjoint held-out split; with $λ= 0.5$, $α$ clears Bonferroni on two of the four (model, budget) cells (Qwen $b{=}128$ and Llama $b{=}64$), no cell is significantly negative, and the pre-registered Branch~A triggers. The finding is asymmetric: a minimal scoring modification beat heavier structural redesigns in this regime, and the combined matched-memory, sympy-graded, held-out confirmation protocol is the evidence standard that made the asymmetry visible.

</details>


### [41] [Policy Optimization in Hybrid Discrete-Continuous Action Spaces via Mixed Gradients](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14297)
*Matias Alvo,Daniel Russo,Yash Kanoria*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出混合策略优化（HPO）方法，通过结合路径梯度和得分函数梯度的混合估计器，解决混合离散-连续动作空间中的强化学习问题，在库存控制和切换线性二次调节器问题上显著优于PPO。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准模型无关的策略梯度方法在高维连续动作空间中存在严重的信用分配问题，而可微模拟在遇到离散动作或非光滑动力学时会产生有偏或无效梯度，现有方法难以有效处理混合离散-连续动作空间。

Method: 提出HPO框架，在平滑区域通过模拟器反向传播，使用结合路径梯度和得分函数梯度的混合梯度估计器保持无偏性；同时将存在动作不连续的问题重新表述为混合形式以扩展适用性。

Result: 在库存控制和切换线性二次调节器问题上，HPO显著优于PPO，且性能差距随连续动作维度增加而扩大；混合梯度的交叉项在离散最优响应附近可忽略，从而允许连续和离散组件近似解耦更新。

Conclusion: HPO有效解决了混合离散-连续动作空间的强化学习问题，通过混合梯度估计实现了更好的梯度质量和样本效率，且在高维场景下优势更为明显。

Abstract: We study reinforcement learning in hybrid discrete-continuous action spaces, such as settings where the discrete component selects a regime (or index) and the continuous component optimizes within it -- a structure common in robotics, control, and operations problems. Standard model-free policy gradient methods rely on score-function (SF) estimators and suffer from severe credit-assignment issues in high-dimensional settings, leading to poor gradient quality. On the other hand, differentiable simulation largely sidesteps these issues by backpropagating through a simulator, but the presence of discrete actions or non-smooth dynamics yields biased or uninformative gradients. To address this, we propose Hybrid Policy Optimization (HPO), which backpropagates through the simulator wherever smoothness permits, using a mixed gradient estimator that combines pathwise and SF gradients while maintaining unbiasedness. We also show how problems with action discontinuities can be reformulated in hybrid form, further broadening its applicability. Empirically, HPO substantially outperforms PPO on inventory control and switched linear-quadratic regulator problems, with performance gaps increasing as the continuous action dimension grows. Finally, we characterize the structure of the mixed gradient, showing that its cross term -- which captures how continuous actions influence future discrete decisions -- becomes negligible near a discrete best response, thereby enabling approximate decentralized updates of the continuous and discrete components and reducing variance near optimality. All resources are available at github.com/MatiasAlvo/hybrid-rl.

</details>

*Qiyuan Chen,Jiayu Zhou,Raed Al Kontar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出语言诱导先验（LIP）框架，利用预训练大语言模型从目标域文本描述中提取先验知识，通过EM算法识别源域相关性，解决了冷启动领域自适应中因目标数据稀缺导致的负迁移问题，并在描述、预测和规定性三类任务上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 冷启动场景下目标域数据极度稀缺，传统统计方法无法有效区分相关与不相关源域，导致负迁移。现有方法忽略了广泛可用但未被充分利用的目标域文本描述资源。

Method: 提出LIP框架：1) 利用预训练LLM从目标域文本描述中提取语义信息，转化为概率先验（LIP）；2) 将LIP集成到EM算法中，在目标信号弱时引导源域选择，随样本积累逐步细化；3) 兼容任意可参数化的似然模型。

Result: 理论上证明在先验正确时LIP的MSE达到oracle水平，且无论LIP质量如何均保持渐近一致性。实验在Gaussian估计、C-MAPSS预测和MuJoCo跳跃控制三个任务上验证了有效性。

Conclusion: LIP框架有效利用LLM从文本描述中提取的语义先验，解决了冷启动域自适应的核心悖论，具有理论保证和广泛适用性。

Abstract: Domain adaptation faces a fundamental paradox in the cold-start regime. When target data is scarce, statistical methods fail to distinguish relevant source domains from irrelevant ones, which often leads to negative transfer. In this paper, we address this challenge by leveraging expert textual descriptions of the target domain, a resource that is often available but overlooked. We propose a probabilistic framework that translates these semantic descriptions into a choice model, namely a Language-Induced Prior (LIP), that learns the preferences from a pretrained Large Language Model (LLM). The LIP is then integrated into an Expectation-Maximization algorithm to identify source relevance. Methodologically, this framework is compatible with any parametric model where a likelihood is available. It allows the LIP to guide the selection of sources when target signals are weak, while gradually refining these choices as samples accumulate. Theoretically, we prove that the estimator roughly matches an oracle cold-start MSE under a correct prior, while remaining asymptotically consistent regardless of the quality of the LIP. Empirically, we validated the framework on a descriptive (Gaussian estimation), a predictive (C-MAPSS dataset), and a prescriptive task (MuJoCo hopper).

</details>


### [43] [Matrix-Space Reinforcement Learning for Reusing Local Transition Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14304)
*Zuyuan Zhang,Carlee Joe-Wong,Tian Lan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Matrix-Space Reinforcement Learning (MSRL)，通过轨迹段的半正定矩阵描述符（聚合一阶和二阶统计量）实现组合泛化，证明其完备性、可加性和最小充分性，并在条件下实现源学习映射到新任务的引导迁移，实验达到0.73 AUC，优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在序列决策的组合泛化中忽略了局部转移几何和动态结构，本文旨在通过几何抽象捕捉轨迹段的隐藏结构，实现高效的技能迁移。

Method: 提出MSRL：用正半定矩阵描述符聚合提升后单步转移的一阶和二阶统计量，在抽象矩阵空间中进行代数组合；证明描述符在坐标规范下的良定义性、对诱导低阶加性信号类的完备性、有效组合下的可加性以及最小充分性；通过将价值函数条件化于轨迹段矩阵，得到动作值的一阶光滑近似，实现源学习的矩阵到值映射引导新任务学习；结合阻塞滤波拒绝不合理组合。

Result: MSRL在有限预算下达到最优平均目标AUC 0.73，优于MSRL从头训练(0.65)、TD-MPC-PT+FT(0.63)和TD-MPC(0.57)。

Conclusion: MSRL作为一种插件兼容的几何抽象方法，有效提升了组合泛化和迁移学习的性能，验证了轨迹段矩阵描述符在强化学习中的理论价值和实践优势。

Abstract: Compositional generalization in sequential decision-making requires identifying which parts of prior rollouts remain useful for new tasks. Existing methods reuse skills or predictive models, but often overlook rich local transition geometry and dynamics. We propose Matrix-Space Reinforcement Learning (MSRL), a geometric abstraction that represents trajectory segments through positive semidefinite matrix descriptors aggregating first- and second-order statistics of lifted one-step transitions. These descriptors expose shared hidden structure, support algebraic composition in an abstract matrix space, and reveal opportunities for transfer. We prove that the descriptor is well defined up to coordinate gauge, complete for the induced low-order additive signal class, additive under valid segment composition, and minimally sufficient among admissible additive descriptors. We further show that conditioning value functions on the trajectory-segment matrix yields a first-order smooth approximation of action values, enabling source-learned matrix-to-value mappings to bootstrap learning in new tasks. MSRL is plug-in compatible with standard model-free and model-based methods, while obstruction filtering rejects implausible compositions. Empirically, MSRL achieves the best average finite-budget target AUC of 0.73, outperforming MSRL from scratch (0.65), TD-MPC-PT+FT (0.63), and TD-MPC (0.57).

</details>


### [44] [Beyond Binary: Reframing GUI Critique as Continuous Semantic Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14311)
*Yuchen Sun,Pei Fu,Shaojie Zhang,Anan Du,Xiuwen Xi,Ruoceng Zhang,Zhenbo Luo,Jian Luan,Chongyang Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出BBCritic，将GUI评分从二分类转变为基于功能等价假设的度量学习，通过两阶段对比学习在层次化功能空间中对齐指令与动作，并构建了首个细粒度排名基准BBBench。3B参数的BBCritic超越7B级的SOTA二分类模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GUI批评模型采用二分类，导致有效动作与看似合理但无效的干扰项评分严重纠缠（Affordance Collapse和Noise Sensitivity），无法实现细粒度排名。

Method: 基于功能等价假设，通过两阶段对比学习（第一阶段学习功能空间结构，第二阶段精细化区分语义等价但功能不同的动作）来训练批评模型，将GUI批评从分类问题重构为度量学习问题。

Result: BBCritic-3B在无需额外标注的情况下，超越7B参数的SOTA二分类模型；在跨平台和跨任务上表现出强零样本迁移能力。

Conclusion: GUI批评本质上是度量学习问题而非分类问题，超越二分类的范式转换可以显著提升测试时扩展的效果。

Abstract: Test-Time Scaling (TTS), which samples multiple candidate actions and ranks them via a Critic Model, has emerged as a promising paradigm for generalist GUI agents. Its efficacy thus hinges on the critic's fine-grained ranking ability. However, existing GUI critic models uniformly adopt binary classification. Our motivational analysis of these models exposes a severe entanglement: scores for valid actions and plausible-but-invalid distractors become indistinguishable. We attribute this failure to two structural defects: Affordance Collapse--the hierarchical affordance space is compressed into 0/1 labels; and Noise Sensitivity--binary objectives overfit to noisy decision boundaries. To resolve this, we introduce BBCritic (Beyond-Binary Critic), a paradigm shift grounded in the Functional Equivalence Hypothesis. Through two-stage contrastive learning, BBCritic aligns instructions and actions in a shared Affordance Space, recovering the hierarchical structure that binary supervision flattens. We also present BBBench (Beyond-Binary Bench), the first GUI critic benchmark that pairs a dense action space with a hierarchical four-level taxonomy, enabling fine-grained ranking evaluation. Experimental results show that BBCritic-3B, trained without any extra annotation, outperforms 7B-parameter SOTA binary models. It demonstrates strong zero-shot transferability across platforms and tasks, supporting our methodological view: GUI critique is fundamentally a metric-learning problem, not a classification one.

</details>


### [45] [Guided Diffusion Sampling for Precipitation Forecast Interventions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14317)
*Ayumu Ueyama,Kazuhiko Kawamoto,Hiroshi Kera*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于梯度引导的扩散采样框架，在扩散式天气预报模型中实现降水减少干预，相比对抗扰动生成更物理合理的干预方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 极端降水造成严重社会经济损失，天气控制作为潜在缓解策略长期被讨论，但基于数据驱动预报模型的扰动式干预尚未被探索。

Method: 提出基于梯度引导的扩散采样框架，不直接扰动大气状态，而是引导扩散采样轨迹实现降水减少，同时保持与大气分布的一致性。从垂直/变量扰动剖面、潜空间轨迹偏差、跨模型可迁移性三个维度评估物理合理性。

Result: 在WeatherBench2极端降水事件上，该方法实现了有效的降水减少，且干预比对抗扰动更物理合理。

Conclusion: 梯度引导的扩散采样框架为数据驱动天气控制提供了一种更物理合理的干预途径。

Abstract: Extreme precipitation causes severe societal and economic damage, and weather control has long been discussed as a potential mitigation strategy. However, to the best of our knowledge, perturbation-based interventions for weather control using data-driven weather forecasting models have not yet been explored. While adversarial attacks also generate perturbations that alter forecasts, they aim to exploit model artifacts and do not account for physical plausibility. In this paper, we propose a gradient-based guidance framework for precipitation-reduction interventions through diffusion sampling in diffusion-based weather forecasting models. Instead of directly perturbing atmospheric states, our method steers the diffusion sampling trajectory, enabling precipitation reduction while maintaining consistency with the atmospheric distribution. To assess physical plausibility, we evaluate from three perspectives: (i) vertical and variable-wise perturbation profiles, (ii) latent-space trajectory deviation, and (iii) cross-model transferability. Experiments on extreme precipitation events from WeatherBench2 demonstrate that our method achieves effective precipitation reduction while yielding more physically plausible interventions than adversarial perturbations.

</details>


### [46] [Dynamic Latent Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14323)
*Fangyuan Yu,Xin Su,Amir Abdullah*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出通用Dijkstra搜索（GDS）框架，证明时变奖励MDP中全局最优策略可通过中间子策略的时间组合恢复。基于此提出动态潜在路由（DLR），一种语言模型后训练方法，通过单阶段动态搜索联合学习离散潜码、路由策略和模型参数。在低数据微调设置中，DLR在四个数据集和六个模型上平均超越监督微调+6.6个百分点，并学习到具有清晰因果角色的结构化路由行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索时变奖励MDP中时间性子策略组合的理论基础，并基于此设计更高效的语言模型后训练方法，以解决低数据场景下监督微调性能不足的问题。

Method: 提出通用Dijkstra搜索（GDS）框架并理论证明最优策略可通过子策略时间组合恢复；基于GDS的"搜索-选择-更新"原则设计动态潜在路由（DLR），在单训练阶段联合学习离散潜码、路由策略和模型参数。

Result: 在四个数据集和六个模型上，DLR在低数据微调设置中平均超越监督微调+6.6个百分点，而先前的离散潜码基线始终低于SFT。机制分析和消融实验表明DLR学习到具有不同因果角色的结构化路由行为。

Conclusion: DLR是一种有效的语言模型后训练方法，在低数据场景下优于传统监督微调，且学习到的结构化路由行为具有可解释的因果机制。

Abstract: We investigate the temporal concatenation of sub-policies in Markov Decision Processes (MDP) with time-varying reward functions. We introduce General Dijkstra Search (GDS), and prove that globally optimal goal-reaching policies can be recovered through temporal composition of intermediate optimal sub-policies. Motivated by the "search, select, update" principle underlying GDS, we propose Dynamic Latent Routing (DLR), a language-model post-training method that jointly learns discrete latent codes, routing policies, and model parameters through dynamic search in a single training stage. In low-data fine-tuning settings, DLR matches or outperforms supervised fine-tuning across four datasets and six models, achieving a mean gain of +6.6 percentage points, while prior discrete-latent baselines consistently underperform SFT. Mechanistic analyses and targeted code ablations show that DLR learns structured routing behaviors with distinct causal roles.

</details>


### [47] [AIM-DDI: A Model-Agnostic Multimodal Integration Module for Drug-Drug Interaction Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14327)
*Yerin Park,Sangseon Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: AIM-DDI提出了一种架构无关的多模态融合模块，通过将异构药物信息表示为共享潜在空间中的token，实现了跨不同DDI预测模型的通用集成，在DrugBank基准上显著提升了药物-药物相互作用预测性能，尤其在双未见药物场景下效果最强。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有DDI多模态模型的融合机制通常与特定预测架构绑定，限制了跨模型复用；同时，未见过药物的泛化（unseen-drug generalization）是DDI预测中的关键挑战。

Method: 将结构、化学、语义等异构药物信号统一表示为共享潜在空间中的token，通过统一的融合模块建模跨模态token之间的依赖关系，实现架构无关的多模态集成。

Result: 在多个DDI模型和DrugBank设置下的评估中，AIM-DDI一致性地提升了预测性能，其中在双未见药物（both-unseen）这一最具挑战性的设置下增益最大。

Conclusion: 将多模态集成视为可复用模块而非模型特定的融合组件，是提升未见过药物DDI预测鲁棒性的有效策略。

Abstract: Drug-drug interaction (DDI) prediction is a critical task in computational biomedicine, as adverse interactions between co-administered drugs can cause severe side effects and clinical risks. A key challenge is unseen-drug generalization, where interactions must be predicted for drugs not observed during training. Although multimodal DDI models exploit diverse drug-related information, their fusion mechanisms are often tied to specific prediction architectures, limiting their reuse across models. To address this, we propose AIM-DDI, an architecture-independent multimodal integration module that represents heterogeneous modality information as tokens in a shared latent space. By modeling dependencies across modality tokens through a unified fusion module, AIM-DDI enables model-agnostic integration of structural, chemical, and semantic drug signals across different DDI prediction architectures. Extensive evaluations across diverse DDI models and DrugBank-based settings show that AIM-DDI consistently improves prediction performance, with the strongest gains under the most challenging both-unseen setting where neither drug in a test pair is observed during training. These results suggest that treating multimodal integration as a reusable module, rather than a model-specific fusion component, is an effective strategy for robust unseen-drug DDI prediction.

</details>


### [48] [Exemplar Partitioning for Mechanistic Interpretability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14347)
*Jessica Rumbelow*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Exemplar Partitioning (EP)，一种无监督方法，通过Voronoi划分和领导者聚类从LLM激活中构建可解释特征字典，相比稀疏自编码器(SAE)所需token减少约1000倍。字典由观测到的样本锚定，大小由激活几何自动决定。实验表明EP区域可解释、支持因果干预，在AxBench任务上AUROC达0.881，接近SAE-A的0.911，计算量减少约1000倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有稀疏自编码器(SAEs)在大型语言模型激活空间中构建可解释特征时计算成本极高（需要大量token），且字典大小需预先指定，跨模型、跨层、跨检查点的字典无法直接比较。作者希望提出一种更高效、计算成本低、无需预指定字典大小且字典可直接比较的无监督方法。

Method: Exemplar Partitioning (EP)：对激活空间进行Voronoi划分，通过领导者聚类（leader-clustering）流式处理激活，在距离阈值内聚类。每个区域由观测到的样本（exemplar）锚定，该样本同时作为区域成员判定标准和干预方向。字典大小不由人工预先指定，而是由给定阈值下的激活几何结构自动确定。由于样本是观测到的而非学习到的，从相同数据流构建的字典可直接跨层、跨模型、跨训练检查点比较。

Result: 在Gemma-2-2B上：1) EP区域可解释且支持因果干预（指令微调后的拒绝行为集中在一个区域，消融该样本可破坏保留的拒绝行为）；2) 跨检查点匹配可分离微调中保留和引入的方向；3) EP区域与SAE特征分解激活空间方式不同，但共享约20%核心区域（F1>0.5）；4) EP one-hot探针在ℓ₀=1时保留原始激活探针约97%的准确率；5) 最近样本距离提供免费的OOD信号；6) 在AxBench latent概念检测任务上，EP在Gemma-2-2B-it L20层达到平均AUROC 0.881，比GemmaScope SAE基线高0.126，比SAE-A的0.911仅低0.030，计算量减少约1000倍。

Conclusion: Exemplar Partitioning是一种高效、可解释的无监督特征字典构建方法，相比SAE在计算效率上有三个数量级的提升，同时保持了良好的可解释性和干预能力，支持跨模型、跨层的直接比较，为LLM可解释性研究提供了新的工具。

Abstract: We introduce Exemplar Partitioning (EP), an unsupervised method for constructing interpretable feature dictionaries from large language model activations with $\sim 10^{3}\times$ fewer tokens than comparable sparse autoencoders (SAEs). An EP dictionary is a Voronoi partition of activation space, built by leader-clustering streamed activations within a distance threshold. Each region is anchored by an observed exemplar that serves as both its membership criterion and intervention direction; dictionary size is not prespecified, but determined by the activation geometry at that threshold. Because exemplars are observed rather than learned, dictionaries built from the same data stream are directly comparable across layers, models, and training checkpoints.
  We characterise EP as an interpretability object via targeted demonstrations of properties newly accessible through this construction, plus one head-to-head benchmark. In Gemma-2-2B, EP dictionary regions are interpretable and support causal interventions: refusal in instruction-tuned Gemma concentrates in a region whose exemplar ablation can collapse held-out refusal. Cross-checkpoint matching between base and instruction-tuned dictionaries separates the directions preserved through finetuning from those introduced by it. EP regions and Gemma Scope SAE features decompose activation space differently but agree on a shared core: $\sim 20\%$ of EP regions match an SAE feature at $F_{1} > 0.5$, and EP one-hot probes retain $\sim 97\%$ of raw-activation probe accuracy at $\ell_{0} = 1$. Nearest-exemplar distance provides a free out-of-distribution signal at inference. On AxBench latent concept detection at Gemma-2-2B-it L20, EP at $p_{1}$ reaches mean AUROC $0.881$, $+0.126$ over the canonical GemmaScope SAE leaderboard entry and within $0.030$ of SAE-A's $0.911$, at $\sim 10^{3}\times$ less build compute.

</details>


### [49] [Distributionally Robust Multi-Task Reinforcement Learning via Adaptive Task Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14350)
*Nicholas E. Corrado,Wenyuan Huang,Josiah P. Hanna*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出DRATS算法，通过自适应任务采样解决多任务强化学习中因数据分配不均导致的难任务学习缓慢问题，将MTRL形式化为可行性问题并优化最差任务回报差距，在MetaWorld基准上提升了数据效率和最差任务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MTRL方法通常平等分配每个任务的环境交互次数，导致简单任务数据过分配而困难任务数据欠分配，造成学习不平衡。作者指出这是一个被忽视但关键的问题，不同于以往关注的梯度冲突问题。

Method: 将MTRL形式化为可行性问题，推导出最小化最差情况回报差距（目标回报与当前回报之差）的极小极大目标，提出DRATS算法自适应地优先采样距离解决最远的任务。

Result: 在MetaWorld-MT10和MT50基准测试中，DRATS相比现有任务采样算法提升了数据效率并增加了最差任务性能。

Conclusion: 数据分配不均是MTRL中一个独立且重要的挑战，通过自适应任务采样可以有效缓解该问题，提升多任务学习的整体效率和均衡性。

Abstract: Multi-task reinforcement learning (MTRL) aims to train a single agent to efficiently optimize performance across multiple tasks simultaneously. However, jointly optimizing all tasks often yields imbalanced learning: agents quickly solve easy tasks but learn slowly on harder ones. While prior work primarily attributes this imbalance to conflicting task gradients and proposes gradient manipulation or specialized architectures to address it, we instead focus on a distinct and under-explored challenge: imbalanced data allocation. Standard MTRL allocates an equal number of environment interactions to each task, which over-allocates data to easy tasks that require relatively few interactions to solve and under-allocates data to hard tasks that require substantially more experience to solve. To address this challenge, we introduce Distributionally Robust Adaptive Task Sampling (DRATS), an algorithm that adaptively prioritizes sampling tasks furthest from being solved. We derive DRATS by formalizing MTRL as a feasibility problem from which we derive a minimax objective for minimizing the worst-case return gap, the difference between a desired target return and the agent's return on a task. In benchmarks like MetaWorld-MT10 and MT50, DRATS improves data efficiency and increases worst-task performance compared to existing task sampling algorithms.

</details>


### [50] [RQ-MoE: Residual Quantization via Mixture of Experts for Efficient Input-Dependent Vector Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14359)
*Zhengjia Zhong,Shuyan Ke,Zaizhou Lin,Jiaqi Song,Hongyi Lan,Hui Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出RQ-MoE框架，通过混合专家机制实现输入自适应的动态码本构建，并采用双流量化解耦指令与量化过程，在保持或超越现有方法性能的同时，实现6-14倍解码加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有矢量量化方法面临两难：静态多码本方法受限于固定码本，难以处理异构数据几何；动态量化器（如QINCo）虽能适应输入但存在严格序列依赖，造成解码瓶颈。

Method: 提出RQ-MoE框架，包含两级MoE（混合专家）与双流量化流：一级MoE用于动态码本构建，另一级用于指令解耦；通过解耦指令与量化过程实现并行解码。

Result: 在重建和检索任务上达到SOTA或相当性能，解码速度比现有方法快6-14倍。

Conclusion: RQ-MoE通过MoE机制实现了高效的动态矢量量化，理论上证明了标准残差量化与QINCo是其约束特例，并为专家维度设置提供了指导原则。

Abstract: Vector quantization is a fundamental tool for compressing high-dimensional embeddings, yet existing multi-codebook methods rely on static codebooks that limit expressiveness under heterogeneous data geometry. While recent dynamic quantizers like QINCo adapt codebooks to individual inputs and improve expressiveness, their strict sequential dependencies create decoding bottlenecks. We propose Residual Quantization via Mixture of Experts (RQ-MoE), a framework combining a two-level MoE with dual-stream quantization to enable input-dependent codebook adaptation for efficient vector quantization. RQ-MoE enables dynamic codebook construction and decouples instruction from quantization, facilitating parallel decoding. Theoretically, we show that standard Residual Quantization and QINCo can be recovered as constrained special cases of RQ-MoE, and derive a guideline for setting expert dimensionality in RQ-MoE. Extensive experiments show that RQ-MoE achieves state-of-the-art or on-par performance in reconstruction and retrieval, while providing 6x-14x faster decoding than prior vector quantization methods. The implementation is available at https://github.com/KDEGroup/RQ-MoE.

</details>


### [51] [MoRe: Modular Representations for Principled Continual Representation Learning on Squantial Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14364)
*Jiaqi Sun,Boyang Sun,Mohamad Rasmy,Xiangchen Song,Kun Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出MoRe框架，通过将知识分解为层次化的基础模块和特定模块，实现选择性更新而不干扰已有表征，在持续学习中改善可塑性-稳定性平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 持续学习的核心挑战是如何在新数据适应与已有知识保留之间取得平衡。现有方法多通过修改参数或架构以监督方式处理任务，但根本问题是表征层面的：需要既保持结构化又能选择性更新的表征，且结构应反映数据内在组织而非任务边界。

Method: 受大脑模块化组织启发，MoRe在表征层面而非架构层面识别模块化。它将知识分解为基础模块和特定模块的层次结构，保证可识别性，在适应过程中实现原则性的模块重用、对齐和扩展，同时通过构造保留旧模块。

Result: 在合成基准和真实世界LLM激活上的实验表明，MoRe产生了可解释的层次化结构，并改善了可塑性-稳定性权衡。

Conclusion: MoRe为持续适应提供了原则性基础框架，通过表征层面的模块化分解有效解决了持续学习中的可塑性-稳定性困境。

Abstract: Continual learning requires models to adapt to new data while preserving previously acquired knowledge. At its core, this challenge can be viewed as principled one-step adaptation: incorporating new information with minimal interference to existing representations. Most existing approaches address this challenge by modifying model parameters or architectures in a supervised, task-specific manner. However, the underlying issue is representational: tasks require distinct yet structured representations that can be selectively updated without disrupting representations, while structure should reflect intrinsic organization in the data rather than task boundaries. In sequential data, time-delayed dependencies provide a natural signal for uncovering this organization, revealing how fundamental representations give rise to more specific ones. Inspired by the modular organization of the human brain, we propose MoRe, a framework that identifies modularity in the representation itself rather than allocating it at the architectural level. MoRe decomposes knowledge into a hierarchy of fundamental and specific modules with identifiability guarantees, enabling principled module reuse, alignment, and expansion during adaptation while preserving old modules by construction. Experiments on synthetic benchmarks and real-world LLM activations demonstrate interpretable hierarchical structure, improved plasticity-stability trade-offs, suggesting MoRe as a principled foundation for continual adaptation

</details>


### [52] [LoMETab: Beyond Rank-1 Ensembles for Tabular Deep Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14365)
*Changryeol Choi,Hyewon Park,Yujin Kwon,Gowun Jeong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LoMETab 提出了一种秩为 r 的乘法隐式集成泛化方法，通过对权重进行 Hadamard 分解（W ⊙ (1 + A_k B_k^T)），引入适配器秩 r 和初始化尺度 σ_init 两个多样性控制轴，实验证明该方法能有效控制成员间的预测多样性，并在分类与回归任务中展现出数据集依赖的性能表现，超越固定秩 1 的 BatchEnsemble/TabM 结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前表格学习基准中主流方法之间性能差距缩小，缺乏清晰层次结构，因此需要理解和控制简单神经表格模型具有竞争力的机制，以超越单纯追求性能提升的范式。

Method: 提出 LoMETab，将秩 1 的 BatchEnsemble/TabM 调制推广到秩 r 的恒等残差 Hadamard 族，每个成员权重参数化为 W_k = W ⊙ (1 + A_k B_k^T)，其中 W 共享，(A_k, B_k) 为成员特异的低秩因子；通过适配器秩 r 和初始化尺度 σ_init 两个轴控制多样性。

Result: 理论上证明当 r ≥ 2 时严格扩大了 BatchEnsemble 的假设类；实验上该方法在分类数据集上维持比加性低秩消融更高的成对 KL 散度；(r, σ_init) 对成对 KL 提供跨越数个数量级的广泛控制；多样性体现在 argmax 不一致性（分类）和歧义性（回归）上；性能在 (r, σ_init) 网格上表现出数据集依赖性。

Conclusion: LoMETab 作为一个可控的隐式集成家族而非固定的秩 1 结构，提供了在表格学习中控制成员多样性的有效手段，性能可根据数据集灵活调节。

Abstract: Recent tabular learning benchmarks increasingly show a tight performance cluster rather than a clear hierarchy among leading methods, spanning gradient boosted decision trees, attention-based architectures, and implicit ensembles such as TabM. As benchmark gains plateau, a complementary goal is to understand and control the mechanisms that make simple neural tabular models competitive. We propose LoMETab, a rank-$r$ generalization of multiplicative implicit ensembles. LoMETab lifts the rank-1 BatchEnsemble/TabM modulation to a rank-$r$ identity-residual Hadamard family by parameterizing each member weight as $W_k = W \odot (1 + A_kB_k^\top)$, where $W$ is shared and $(A_k, B_k)$ are member-specific low-rank factors. This exposes two practical diversity-control axes: the adapter rank $r$ and the initialization scale $σ_{\mathrm{init}}$, and we prove that for $r \ge 2$ this generalization strictly enlarges BatchEnsemble's hypothesis class. Empirically, we show that this added capacity manifests as measurable predictive diversity after training: on representative classification datasets, LoMETab sustains higher pairwise KL than an additive low-rank ablation, and $(r, σ_{\mathrm{init}})$ provides broad control over pairwise KL, varying by up to several orders of magnitude across configurations. The induced diversity is reflected in task-appropriate output-level measures: argmax disagreement for classification and ambiguity for regression, indicating that the control extends beyond pairwise KL to decision- and output-level member variation. Finally, experiments sweeping over adapter rank $r$ and initialization scale $σ_{\mathrm{init}}$ reveal that predictive performance is dataset-dependent over the $(r, σ_{\mathrm{init}})$ grid, supporting LoMETab as a controllable family of implicit ensembles rather than a fixed rank-1 construction.

</details>


### [53] [Turning Stale Gradients into Stable Gradients: Coherent Coordinate Descent with Implicit Landscape Smoothing for Lightweight Zeroth-Order Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14373)
*Chen Liang,Xiatao Sun,Qian Wang,Daniel Rakita*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 Coherent Coordinate Descent (CoCD)，一种确定性、样本高效且预算感知的零阶优化器。CoCD 通过利用梯度相干性将历史梯度转化为计算资产，实现每步 O(1) 查询复杂度，同时保持全局下降方向。实验表明，CoCD 在样本效率、收敛稳定性和精度上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有零阶优化方法面临两难困境：标准有限差分采样效率低，随机子空间方法方差高。需要一种确定性、样本高效且稳定的零阶优化方法，以支持反向传播不可用场景（如内存受限的端侧学习和黑箱优化）。

Method: 提出 Coherent Coordinate Descent (CoCD)，一种确定性零阶优化器。理论形式化梯度相干性概念，证明 CoCD 等价于带"热启动"的块循环坐标下降（BCCD），将历史梯度从负担转化为计算资产，实现每步 O(1) 查询复杂度。此外，分析了更大有限差分步长通过降低有效平滑常数带来的隐式平滑效应。

Result: 在 MLP、CNN 和 ResNet（最高 27 万参数）上，CoCD 在样本效率和收敛损失/精度上显著优于 BCCD，且比随机零阶方法具有更好的稳定性。

Conclusion: 确定性、结构感知的更新为轻量级零阶优化提供了一种优于随机化的替代方案。

Abstract: Zeroth-Order (ZO) optimization is pivotal for scenarios where backpropagation is unavailable, such as memory-constrained on-device learning and black-box optimization. However, existing methods face a stark trade-off: they are either sample-inefficient (e.g., standard finite differences) or suffer from high variance due to randomized estimation (e.g., random subspace methods). In this work, we propose Coherent Coordinate Descent (CoCD), a deterministic, sample-efficient, and budget-aware ZO optimizer. Theoretically, we formalize the notion of gradient coherence and demonstrate that CoCD is equivalent to Block Cyclic Coordinate Descent (BCCD) with ``warm starts,'' effectively converting historical (stale) gradients from a liability into a computational asset. This mechanism enables $O(1)$ query complexity per step while maintaining global descent directions. Furthermore, we derive error bounds revealing a counter-intuitive insight: larger finite-difference step sizes can induce an implicit smoothing effect on the optimization landscape by reducing the effective smoothness constant, thereby improving convergence stability. Experiments on MLP, CNN, and ResNet architectures (up to 270k parameters) demonstrate that CoCD significantly outperforms BCCD in terms of sample efficiency and convergence loss/accuracy, and exhibits superior stability over randomized ZO methods. Our results suggest that deterministic, structure-aware updates offer a superior alternative to randomization for lightweight ZO optimization.

</details>


### [54] [Optimal Pattern Detection Tree for Symbolic Rule-Based Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14374)
*Young-Chae Hong,Yangho Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于混合整数规划的最优模式检测树（OPDT），用于通过二元分类在数据中发现单一最优模式，并引入分支结构约束（BSC）框架以整合领域知识，在中等规模数据集上实现有最优性保证的模式发现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统黑箱深度学习模型缺乏可解释性，而符号规则发现能生成人类可理解的规则，在医疗、风险评估等需要透明性和可解释性的领域至关重要。

Method: 提出OPDT模型，基于新型混合整数规划进行二元分类以发现数据中的最优模式；引入BSC框架，允许决策者将领域知识和约束直接编码到模型中；通过最大化覆盖率和最小化误报率的目标函数来识别隐藏模式。

Result: 计算实验表明，OPDT能够在中等规模数据集上、在合理运行时间内，以最优性保证发现模式。

Conclusion: OPDT作为一种基于优化的规则发现方法，能够以可证明的最优性保证发现数据中的隐藏模式，同时通过BSC框架支持领域知识注入，兼顾了可解释性与最优性。

Abstract: Pattern discovery in data plays a crucial role across diverse domains, including healthcare, risk assessment, and machinery maintenance. In contrast to black-box deep learning models, symbolic rule discovery emerges as a key data mining task, generating human-interpretable rules that offer both transparency and intuitive explainability. This paper introduces the Optimal Pattern Detection Tree (OPDT), a rule-based machine learning model based on novel mixed-integer programming to discover a single optimal pattern in data through binary classification. To incorporate prior knowledge and compliance requirements, we further introduce the Branching Structure Constraints (BSC) framework, which enables decision makers to encode domain knowledge and constraints directly into the model. This optimization-based approach discovers a hidden underlying pattern in datasets, when it exists, by identifying an optimal rule that maximizes coverage while minimizing the false positive rate due to misclassification. Our computational experiments show that OPDT discovers a pattern with optimality guarantees on moderately sized datasets within reasonable runtime.

</details>


### [55] [Data-Augmented Game Starts for Accelerating Self-Play Exploration in Imperfect Information Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14379)
*JB Lanier,Nathan Monette,Pierre Baldi,Roy Fox*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为DAGS的多智能体起始状态采样策略，通过利用离线人类演示数据初始化强化学习数据收集的中间状态，显著加速了大规模不完全信息双人零和博弈中的正则化策略梯度方法的在线探索，在固定计算预算下实现了更低的利用度，并提出了一种基于多任务观测标志的偏差缓解方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决大规模不完全信息竞争博弈（如StarCraft、Dota等）中因稀疏奖励和长时域探索困难导致的计算不可行性问题，旨在加速正则化策略梯度方法在双人零和博弈中的在线探索。

Method: 提出DAGS方法，从离线人类演示数据中采样中间状态作为强化学习数据收集的起始点，以促进对策略相关子游戏的探索；同时引入多任务观测标志来缓解因偏置起始状态分布导致的均衡偏差。

Result: 在合成数据集和可解析处理的长期控制变体（包括Kuhn Poker、Goofspiel及一个用于惩罚隐藏信息偏置信念的反例游戏）上，DAGS在固定计算预算下使正则化策略梯度方法实现了更低的利用度，显著提升了探索挑战。

Conclusion: DAGS能有效加速不完全信息博弈中的探索，但偏置起始状态分布可能导致均衡偏差，多任务观测标志提供了一种简单的缓解方案；此外，论文发布了新的基准环境以增加探索难度和状态数量。

Abstract: Finding approximate equilibria for large-scale imperfect-information competitive games such as StarCraft, Dota, and CounterStrike remains computationally infeasible due to sparse rewards and challenging exploration over long horizons. In this paper, we propose a multi-agent starting-state sampling strategy designed to substantially accelerate online exploration in regularized policy-gradient game methods for two-player zero-sum (2p0s) games. Motivated by an assumption that offline demonstrations from skilled humans can provide good coverage of high-level strategies relevant to equilibrium play, we propose the initialization of reinforcement learning data collection at intermediate states sampled from offline data to facilitate exploration of strategically relevant subgames. Referring to this method as Data-Augmented Game Starts (DAGS), we perform experiments using synthetic datasets and analytically tractable, long-horizon control variants of two-player Kuhn Poker, Goofspiel, and a counterexample game designed to penalize biased beliefs over hidden information. Under fixed computational budgets, DAGS enables regularized policy gradient methods to achieve lower exploitability in games with significantly more challenging exploration. We show that augmenting starting state distributions when solving imperfect information games can lead to biased equilibria, and we provide a straightforward mitigation to this in the form of multi-task observation flags. Finally, we release a new set of benchmark environments that drastically increase exploration challenges and state counts in existing OpenSpiel games while keeping exploitability measurements analytically tractable.

</details>


### [56] [NodeSynth: Socially Aligned Synthetic Data for AI Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14381)
*Qazi Mamunur Rashid,Xuan Yang,Zhengzhe Yang,Yanzhou Pan,Erin van Liemt,Darlene Neal,Kshitij Pancholi,Jamila Smith-Loud*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: NodeSynth是一种基于真实世界证据的合成查询生成方法，通过微调的分类器（TaG）生成社会相关的合成查询。评估表明，该方法可使主流LLM的失败率比人类编写的基准高出五倍，消融研究证实细粒度分类扩展是驱动失败率的关键因素，独立验证揭示了Llama-Guard-3等防护模型的严重缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成式AI虽然能大规模生成合成数据用于模型评估，但缺乏针对敏感领域的社会技术细微差别，难以评估模型在社会敏感场景下的表现。

Method: 提出NodeSynth方法论，核心组件为基于真实世界证据微调的分类生成器（TaG），通过细粒度分类扩展生成具有社会相关性的合成查询。

Result: 在四个主流LLM（如Claude 4.5 Haiku）上评估，NodeSynth诱发的失败率比人类编写的基准高出五倍；消融研究证实细粒度分类扩展显著驱动失败率；独立验证发现Llama-Guard-3等防护模型存在关键缺陷。

Conclusion: NodeSynth能够实现可扩展的高风险模型评估，并支持目标安全干预；已开源端到端研究原型和数据集。

Abstract: Recent advancements in generative AI facilitate large-scale synthetic data generation for model evaluation. However, without targeted approaches, these datasets often lack the sociotechnical nuance required for sensitive domains. We introduce NodeSynth, an evidence-grounded methodology that generates socially relevant synthetic queries by leveraging a fine-tuned taxonomy generator (TaG) anchored in real-world evidence. Evaluated against four mainstream LLMs (e.g., Claude 4.5 Haiku), NodeSynth elicited failure rates up to five times higher than human-authored benchmarks. Ablation studies confirm that our granular taxonomic expansion significantly drives these failure rates, while independent validation reveals critical deficiencies in prominent guard models (e.g., Llama-Guard-3). We open-source our end-to-end research prototype and datasets to enable scalable, high-stakes model evaluation and targeted safety interventions (https://github.com/google-research/nodesynth).

</details>


### [57] [GeoViSTA: Geospatial Vision-Tabular Transformer for Multimodal Environment Representation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14406)
*Yuhao Liu,Sadeer Al-Kindi,Ashok Veeraraghavan,Guha Balakrishnan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GeoViSTA提出了一种视觉-表格联合架构，通过双向交叉注意力和地理感知机制，从配准的栅格影像和表格数据中学习统一的地理空间嵌入，并在自监督联合掩码自动编码目标下训练，在疾病死亡率预测和火灾风险预测等下游任务中优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有地理空间基础模型主要处理遥感影像，无法直接建模通常以表格形式存储的结构化社会经济协变量，导致模态鸿沟，限制了模型捕捉完整环境信息以推理复杂环境、社会和健康相关结果的能力。

Method: 提出GeoViSTA视觉-表格Transformer架构，利用双向交叉注意力在模态间交换空间和语义信息，并引入地理感知注意力机制将连续图像块与不规则的人口普查区令牌对齐，采用自监督联合掩码自动编码目标进行训练。

Result: GeoViSTA的统一嵌入在疾病特异性死亡率和火灾危险频率预测等高影响下游任务的线性探测中优于基线方法，且在未见过的区域上表现良好。

Conclusion: 联合建模物理环境与结构化社会经济背景能够产生高度可迁移的表示，实现更全面的地理空间推理。

Abstract: Large-scale pretraining on Earth observation imagery has yielded powerful representations of the natural and built environment. However, most existing geospatial foundation models do not directly model the structured socioeconomic covariates typically stored in tabular form. This modality gap limits their ability to capture the complete total environment, which is critical for reasoning about complex environmental, social, and health-related outcomes. In this work, we propose GeoViSTA (Geospatial Vision-Tabular Transformer), a vision-tabular architecture that learns unified geospatial embeddings from co-registered gridded imagery and tabular data. GeoViSTA utilizes bilateral cross-attention to exchange spatial and semantic information across modalities, guided by a geography-aware attention mechanism that aligns continuous image patches with irregular census-tract tokens. We train GeoViSTA with a self-supervised joint masked-autoencoding objective, forcing it to recover missing image patches and tabular rows using local spatial context and cross-modal cues. Empirically, GeoViSTA's unified embeddings improve linear probing performance on high-impact downstream tasks, outperforming baselines in predicting disease-specific mortality and fire hazard frequency across held-out regions. These results demonstrate that jointly modeling the physical environment alongside structured socioeconomic context yields highly transferable representations for holistic geospatial inference.

</details>


### [58] [What if Tomorrow is the World Cup Final? Counterfactual Time Series Forecasting with Textual Conditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14422)
*Shuqi Gu,Yongxiang Zhao,Baoyu Jing,Kan Ren*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了反事实时间序列预测任务，引入文本条件实现灵活的条件感知预测，并设计了包含事实与反事实场景的综合评估框架，以及一种区分可变/不可变因素的文本归因机制来提升预测精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统时间序列预测方法仅依赖历史数据或事实性未来条件，忽视了反事实场景；现有方法受限于简单结构化条件，难以泛化到现实世界的复杂性。因此需要一种能动态适应未来复杂随机条件、支持反事实推理的预测框架。

Method: 1) 提出反事实时间序列预测任务，引入文本条件（textual conditions）；2) 设计涵盖事实与反事实设置的综合评估框架，可在无真实时间序列数据的情况下进行评估；3) 提出新颖的文本归因机制（text-attribution mechanism），区分可变的（mutable）与不可变的（immutable）因素，提升在复杂随机文本条件下的预测准确性。

Result: 提出的方法能够有效处理事实与反事实场景下的时间序列预测，在缺乏真实时间序列数据时仍可进行评估，并且文本归因机制显著提升了在复杂随机条件下的预测精度。

Conclusion: 反事实时间序列预测结合文本条件是一种有前景的方向，所提出的评估框架和文本归因机制为应对现实世界中的复杂预测挑战提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Time series forecasting has become increasingly critical in real-world scenarios, where future sequences are influenced not only by historical patterns but also by forthcoming events. In this context, forecasting must dynamically adapt to complex and stochastic future conditions, which introduces fundamental challenges in both forecasting and evaluation. Traditional methods typically rely on historical data or factual future conditions, while overlooking counterfactual scenarios. Furthermore, many existing approaches are restricted to simple structured conditions, limiting their ability to generalize to the real-world complexities. To address these gaps, we introduce the task of counterfactual time series forecasting with textual conditions, enabling more flexible and condition-aware forecasting. We propose a comprehensive evaluation framework that encompasses both factual and counterfactual settings, even in the absence of ground truth time series. Additionally, we present a novel text-attribution mechanism that distinguishes mutable from immutable factors, thereby improving forecast accuracy under sophisticated and stochastic textual conditions. The project page is at https://seqml.github.io/TADiff/

</details>


### [59] [Collaborative Yet Personalized Policy Training: Single-Timescale Federated Actor-Critic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14423)
*Leo Muxing Wang,Pengkun Yang,Lili Su*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种联邦演员-评论家框架，智能体共享公共线性子空间表示同时保持个性化局部策略组件，并证明了在异质性马尔可夫轨迹下的有限时间收敛性，实现了关于智能体数量的线性加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作要么忽略环境异质性，要么完全放弃个性化而训练单一共享策略。作者希望同时实现异构环境下的协同策略训练与个性化保留。

Method: 提出联邦演员-评论家框架：智能体共享公共线性子空间表示，同时保持个性化局部策略组件；采用单时间尺度更新与马尔可夫采样；通过投影子空间更新和QR分解的扰动分析、异质性马尔可夫噪声的条件混合论证、以及策略更新与时间依赖的精细差异刻画来建立收敛性。

Result: 评论家误差以$\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(1/((1-γ)^4\sqrt{TK}))$收敛到零，策略梯度范数以$\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(1/((1-γ)^6\sqrt{TK}))$收敛到零，实现了对智能体数量K的线性加速。在联邦Hopper-v5实验上优于Single PPO和FedAvg PPO。

Conclusion: 所提联邦演员-评论家框架能在异构马尔可夫轨迹下实现收敛，且具有线性加速特性；共享子空间带来的迁移学习能力在实验中得到了验证。

Abstract: Despite the popularity of the actor-critic method and the practical needs of collaborative policy training, existing works typically either overlook environmental heterogeneity or give up personalization altogether by training a single shared policy across all agents. We consider a federated actor-critic framework in which agents share a common linear subspace representation while maintaining personalized local policy components, and agents iteratively estimate the common subspace, local critic heads, and local policies (i.e., actors). Under canonical single-timescale updates with Markovian sampling, we establish finite-time convergence via a novel joint linear approximation framework. Specifically, we show that the critic error converges to zero at the rate of $\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(1/((1-γ)^4\sqrt{TK}))$, and the policy gradient norm converges to zero at the rate of $\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(1/((1-γ)^6\sqrt{TK}))$, where $T$ is the number of rounds, $K$ is the number of agents, and $γ\in (0,1)$ is the discount factor. These results demonstrate linear speedup with respect to the number of agents $K$, despite heterogeneous Markovian trajectories under distinct transition kernels and coupled learning dynamics. To address these challenges, we develop a new perturbation analysis for the projected subspace updates and QR decomposition steps, together with conditional mixing arguments for heterogeneous Markovian noise. Furthermore, to handle the additional complications induced by policy updates and temporal dependence, we establish fine-grained characterizations of the discrepancies between function evaluations under Markovian sampling and under temporally frozen policies. Experiments instantiate the framework within PPO on federated \texttt{Hopper-v5} action-map heterogeneity, showing gains over Single PPO and FedAvg PPO and downstream transfer from the learned shared trunk.

</details>


### [60] [FrontierSmith: Synthesizing Open-Ended Coding Problems at Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14445)
*Runyuan He,Qiuyang Mang,Shang Zhou,Kaiyuan Liu,Hanchen Li,Huanzhi Mao,Qizheng Zhang,Zerui Li,Bo Peng,Lufeng Cheng,Tianfu Fu,Yichuan Wang,Wenhao Chai,Jingbo Shang,Alex Dimakis,Joseph E. Gonzalez,Alvin Cheung*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出FrontierSmith系统，能够从现有的封闭式编程任务（如竞赛编程题）自动演化生成开放式编程问题，用于训练更强的LLM编码器。系统通过改变目标、限制输出、泛化输入生成候选变体，利用量化分歧度指标筛选能激发不同解法的问题，并自动生成测试用例与验证器。在Qwen3.5-9B和Qwen3.5-27B上实验表明，训练后模型在FrontierCS和ALE-bench两个基准上分别提升+8.82/+306.36和+12.12/+309.12，且问题使智能体消耗更多tokens和回合数，接近人工设计的数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实编码挑战往往是开放性的，不存在已知最优解，而现有LLM编码研究聚焦于明确定义的任务（实现功能、修bug、竞赛编程）。开放式编码仍是LLM的薄弱环节，主要因为开放式训练问题稀缺且人工构造成本高昂。本文旨在大规模合成开放式编程问题，以训练更强的LLM编码器。

Method: 提出FrontierSmith自动化系统：1）从现有封闭式竞赛编程题出发，通过改变问题目标、限制输出、泛化输入三种方式生成开放式的候选变体；2）使用量化的想法分歧度（idea divergence metric）筛选能促使不同求解器产生多样化解法的问题；3）为筛选出的候选问题自动生成测试用例和验证器。

Result: 在FrontierCS基准上，Qwen3.5-9B提升+8.82分，Qwen3.5-27B提升+12.12分；在ALE-bench上，Qwen3.5-9B提升+306.36（Elo评分），Qwen3.5-27B提升+309.12。合成数据使模型产生更多交互回合和token消耗，与人工构建的数据表现一致。

Conclusion: 封闭式编程问题可以作为生成开放式长程编码训练数据的实用起点，通过自动化演化可有效缓解开放式训练数据稀缺问题，显著提升LLM在开放式编码任务上的表现。

Abstract: Many real-world coding challenges are open-ended and admit no known optimal solution. Yet, recent progress in LLM coding has focused on well-defined tasks such as feature implementation, bug fixing, and competitive programming. Open-ended coding remains a weak spot for LLMs, largely because open-ended training problems are scarce and expensive to construct. Our goal is to synthesize open-ended coding problems at scale to train stronger LLM coders. We introduce FrontierSmith, an automated system for iteratively evolving open-ended problems from existing closed-ended coding tasks. Starting from competitive programming problems, FrontierSmith generates candidate open-ended variants by changing the problems'goals, restricting outputs, and generalizing inputs. It then uses a quantitative idea divergence metric to select problems that elicit genuinely diverse approaches from different solvers. Agents then generate test cases and verifiers for the surviving candidates. On two open-ended coding benchmarks, training on our synthesized data yields substantial gains over the base models: Qwen3.5-9B improves by +8.82 score on FrontierCS and +306.36 (Elo-rating-based performance) on ALE-bench; Qwen3.5-27B improves by +12.12 and +309.12, respectively. The synthesized problems also make agents take more turns and use more tokens, similar to human-curated ones, suggesting that closed-ended seeds can be a practical starting point for long-horizon coding data.

</details>


### [61] [When Answers Stray from Questions: Hallucination Detection via Question-Answer Orthogonal Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14449)
*Siyang Yao,Erhu Feng,Yubin Xia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: QAOD是一个单通道的幻觉检测框架，通过对答案表征进行问题-答案正交分解，去除问题方向分量以抑制领域条件变化，并利用多样性惩罚的Fisher评分选择层和Fisher重要性选择神经元。联合探测策略（正交分量+问题上下文）最大化域内可区分性，仅正交分量探测策略保留领域无关的事实信号以实现鲁棒迁移。联合探测在域内AUROC上达到最佳，仅正交探测在OOD迁移上超越最优白盒基线最高达21%，生成成本低于25%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 黑盒一致性方法需要多次推理，效率低；白盒探针方法虽高效但将答案表征孤立处理，在域迁移下性能显著下降。现有方法难以同时兼顾域内检测精度与跨域泛化鲁棒性。

Method: 提出QAOD框架：1) 对答案表征进行问题-答案正交分解，投影去除问题对齐方向，获得问题正交分量以抑制领域条件变化；2) 通过多样性惩罚的Fisher评分选择信息层，通过Fisher重要性选择判别性神经元；3) 设计两种互补探针策略：联合探针（正交分量+问题上下文）最大化域内可区分性，仅正交探针（仅正交分量）保留领域无关事实信号实现鲁棒迁移。

Result: 联合探针在所有评估的模型-数据集对上实现最佳域内AUROC；仅正交探针在OOD迁移上表现最强，在BioASQ上超越最优白盒基线最高达21%，生成成本低于25%。

Conclusion: QAOD通过正交分解有效分离了问题相关与事实相关信号，实现了域内高精度检测与跨域鲁棒泛化的兼顾，为白盒幻觉检测提供了高效且鲁棒的解决方案。

Abstract: Hallucination detection in large language models (LLMs) requires balancing accu racy, efficiency, and robustness to distribution shift. Black-box consistency methods are effective but demand repeated inference; single-pass white-box probes are effi cient yet treat answer representations in isolation, often degrading sharply under domain shift. We propose QAOD (Question-Answer Orthogonal Decomposition), a single-pass framework that projects away the question-aligned direction from the answer representation to obtain a question-orthogonal component that suppresses domain-conditioned variation. To identify informative signals, QAOD further selects layers via diversity-penalized Fisher scoring and discriminative neurons via Fisher importance. To address both in-domain detection and cross-domain generalization, we design two complementary probing strategies: pairing the or thogonal component with question context yields a joint probe that maximizes in-domain discriminability, while using the orthogonal component alone preserves domain-agnostic factuality signals for robust transfer. QAOD's joint probe achieves the best in-domain AUROC across all evaluated model-dataset pairs, while the orthogonal-only probe delivers the strongest OOD transfer, surpassing the best white-box baseline by up to 21% on BioASQ at under 25% of generation cost.

</details>


### [62] [Focused PU learning from imbalanced data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14467)
*Elias Zavitsanos,Georgios Paliouras*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种针对高度不平衡数据集的PU学习方法，使用聚焦经验风险估计器训练二分类器，在SCAR和SAR两种标注机制下均取得最优性能，并在财务错报检测中验证了实际价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实中的PU学习场景（如疾病基因识别、定向营销、欺诈检测）常面临高度不平衡问题，且正样本难以与负样本区分。现有PU学习方法很少针对不平衡设置或难以检测的正样本进行优化。

Method: 提出一种聚焦经验风险估计器（focused empirical risk estimator），同时利用正样本和无标签样本训练二分类器，专门处理高度不平衡数据集。

Result: 在不平衡数据集上，在SCAR（完全随机选择）和SAR（随机选择）两种标注机制下均达到最先进水平，并在金融错报检测任务中验证了实际效果。

Conclusion: 该方法有效解决了PU学习中的不平衡和难以检测正样本问题，具有广泛的应用前景。

Abstract: We propose a new method of learning from positive and unlabeled (PU) examples in highly imbalanced datasets. Many real-world problems, such as disease gene identification, targeted marketing, fraud detection, and recommender systems, are hard to address with machine learning methods, due to limited labeled data. Often, training data comprises positive and unlabeled instances, the latter typically being dominated by negative, but including also several positive instances. While PU learning is well-studied, few methods address imbalanced settings or hard-to-detect positive examples that resemble negative ones. Our approach uses a focused empirical risk estimator, incorporating both positive and unlabeled examples to train binary classifiers. Empirical evaluations demonstrate state-of-the-art performance on imbalanced datasets under two labeling mechanisms - selecting positives completely at random (SCAR) and selecting at random (SAR). Beyond these controlled experiments, we demonstrate the value of the proposed method in the real-world application of financial misstatement detection.

</details>


### [63] [Enjoy Your Layer Normalization with the Computational Efficiency of RMSNorm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14521)
*Yuxin Guo,Yihao Yue,Yunhao Ni,Yizhou Ruan,Jie Luo,Wenjun Wu,Lei Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种框架，通过将Layer Normalization（LN）的中心化操作折叠到上游线性层中，在不改变模型函数的前提下，将LN替换为更高效的RMSNorm，实现2%-12%的推理加速，且不影响预测结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Layer Normalization（LN）中的逐样本中心化和缩放操作带来了不可忽略的推理开销，而RMSNorm虽然移除了中心化操作提升了效率，但可能损失中心化带来的收益。因此需要一种方法在不损失模型性能的前提下用RMSNorm替代LN。

Method: 提出通过列中心约束（CCC）和基于列的权重中心化（CBWC）将LN的中心化操作折叠到上游的一般线性层中；定义了可折叠LN的概念，并开发了基于图的检测算法来判断任意DNN中的LN是否可被RMSNorm等价替换。

Result: 多个广泛使用的架构中存在大量可折叠LN，可实现精确的推理时转换，端到端加速2%-12%，且不改变模型预测结果；在实际训练中部分等价性被打破时，该方法仍与原始LN竞争力相当，同时提升了效率。

Conclusion: LN的中心化操作在很多情况下可以无损地折叠到上游线性层中，从而安全地替换为RMSNorm以提升推理效率，是一种实用且通用的加速方法。

Abstract: Layer normalization (LN) is a fundamental component in modern deep learning, but its per-sample centering and scaling introduce non-negligible inference overhead. RMSNorm improves efficiency by removing the centering operation, yet this may discard benefits associated with centering. This paper propose a framework to determine whether an LN in an arbitrary DNN can be replaced by RMSNorm without changing the model function. The key idea is to fold LN's centering operation into upstream general linear layers by enforcing zero-mean outputs through the column-centered constraint (CCC) and column-based weight centering (CBWC). We extend the analysis to arbitrary DNNs, define such LNs as foldable LNs, and develop a graph-based detection algorithm. Our analysis shows that many LNs in widely used architectures are foldable, enabling exact inference-time conversion and end-to-end acceleration of 2% to 12% without changing model predictions. Experiments across multiple task families further show that, when exact equivalence is partially broken in practical training settings, our method remains competitive with vanilla LN while improving efficiency.

</details>


### [64] [Lang2MLIP: End-to-End Language-to-Machine Learning Interatomic Potential Development with Autonomous Agentic Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14527)
*Wenwen Li,Yuki Orimo,Nontawat Charoenphakdee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Lang2MLIP，一个基于多智能体和大语言模型的框架，将自然语言输入的机器学习原子间势（MLIP）开发转化为顺序决策问题，无需预定义流程即可自动完成端到端开发。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLIP自动化流程通常假设固定阶段序列或依赖领域专家，缺乏对异质材料系统的适应性，非专家难以使用。

Method: 构建多智能体框架Lang2MLIP，由LLM驱动的决策智能体在每个步骤观察当前数据集、模型、评估结果和执行日志，自动选择合适动作改进模型，支持自我修正和回溯。

Result: 在具有多组分和多界面的固态电解质界面（SEI）系统上验证了该方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 基于LLM的多智能体系统是自动化MLIP开发并降低其使用门槛的有前景方向。

Abstract: Developing machine learning interatomic potentials (MLIPs) for complex materials systems remains challenging because it requires expertise in atomistic simulations, machine learning, and workflow design, as well as iterative active learning procedures. Existing automated pipelines typically assume a fixed sequence of stages or depend on domain experts, which limits their adaptability to heterogeneous materials systems where the optimal curriculum is not known in advance. To lower the barrier to developing MLIPs for non-experts, we propose Lang2MLIP, a multi-agent framework that takes natural-language input and formulates end-to-end MLIP development as a sequential decision-making problem solved by large language models (LLMs). At each step, a decision-making agent observes the current dataset, model, evaluation results, and execution log, and then automatically selects an appropriate action to improve the model. This removes the need for a predefined pipeline and enables the agent to self-correct by revisiting earlier subsystems when new failures arise. We evaluate this approach on a solid electrolyte interphase (SEI) system with multiple components and interfaces. These results suggest that LLM-based multi-agent systems are a promising direction for automating MLIP development and making it more accessible to non-experts.

</details>


### [65] [Exploring Geographic Relative Space in Large Language Models through Activation Patching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14535)
*Stef De Sabbata,Rahul Baiju,Stefano Mizzaro,Kevin Roitero*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究通过机制可解释性中的激活修补方法，探究大型语言模型如何处理相对地理空间，揭示了LLMs在地理应用中存在的安全隐患。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLMs在地理学中应用日益广泛，但我们对这些模型的内部工作机制了解甚少，这引发了关于模型安全性的重大关切。

Method: 采用激活修补（activation patching）这一机制可解释性工具，分析LLMs处理相对地理空间的内部机制。

Result: 揭示了LLMs在理解相对地理空间时的内部处理模式，识别了关键激活区域对空间推理的贡献。

Conclusion: 对LLMs地理空间处理机制的初步理解有助于评估和改善这类模型在地理应用中的安全性。

Abstract: The increased use of Large Language Models (LLMs) in geography raises substantial questions about the safety of integrating these tools across a wide range of processes and analyses, given our very limited understanding of their inner workings. In this extended abstract, we examine how LLMs process relative geographic space using activation patching, an emerging tool for mechanistic interpretability.

</details>


### [66] [RxEval: A Prescription-Level Benchmark for Evaluating LLM Medication Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14543)
*Shuhao Chen,Weisen Jiang,Changmiao Wang,Xiaoqing Wu,Xuanren Shi,Yu Zhang,James T. Kwok*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: RxEval是一个针对住院患者处方推荐的基准测试，通过1,547道多选题评估16个LLM的处方能力，结果显示最佳精确匹配率仅46.10%，前沿模型仍会忽略患者信息并无法得出临床结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有住院药物推荐基准仅以入院级别预测粗粒度药物代码，采用多热诊断和程序代码作为输入，未能模拟真实处方过程中每个时间点的信息丰富性和动态变化，无法准确评估大语言模型的处方能力。

Method: 构建RxEval基准，采用多选题形式评估LLM处方能力。每道题提供详细的患者画像和时间排序的临床轨迹，要求模型从真实处方和通过推理链扰动生成的患者特异性干扰项中，选择具体的药物-剂量-给药途径三元组。基准包含1,547个问题，覆盖584名患者、18个诊断类别和969种独特药物。

Result: 对16个LLM的评估显示，F1分数范围从45.18到77.10，最佳精确匹配率仅为46.10%。前沿模型仍会忽略患者信息并无法推导出临床结论。

Conclusion: RxEval具有挑战性和区分度，能够有效评估LLM的处方能力，但现有模型在复杂临床决策中仍有显著不足。

Abstract: Inpatient medication recommendation requires clinicians to repeatedly select specific medications, doses, and routes as a patient's condition evolves. Existing benchmarks formulate this task as admission-level prediction over coarse drug codes with multi-hot diagnostic and procedure code inputs, failing to capture the per-timepoint, information-rich nature of real prescribing. We propose RxEval, a prescription-level benchmark that evaluates LLM prescribing capability by multiple-choice questions: each question presents a detailed patient profile and time-ordered clinical trajectory, requiring selection of specific medication-dose-route triples from real prescriptions and patient-specific distractors generated via reasoning-chain perturbation. RxEval comprises 1,547 questions spanning 584 patients, 18 diagnostic categories, and 969 unique medications. Evaluation of 16 LLMs shows that RxEval is both challenging and discriminative: F1 ranges from 45.18 to 77.10 across models, and the best Exact Match is only 46.10%. Error analysis reveals that even frontier models may overlook stated patient information and fail to derive clinical conclusions.

</details>


### [67] [Multi-Dimensional Model Integrity and Responsibility Assessment Index and Scoring Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14550)
*Phuc Truong Loc Nguyen,Thanh Hung Do,Truong Thanh Hung Nguyen,Hung Cao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MIRAI（模型完整性与责任评估指数），一个统一评估框架，用于在可解释性、公平性、鲁棒性、隐私性和可持续性五个维度上综合评估表格数据模型，并通过单一聚合分数实现模型间比较。实验表明，更高预测性能并不必然意味着更好的整体完整性和责任性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前高风险表格数据领域仍主要依赖预测性能评估模型，而可解释性、公平性、鲁棒性、隐私性和可持续性等重要维度大多被孤立评估，缺乏统一的综合评价框架。

Method: MIRAI框架将五个维度的已有指标通过归一化和方向对齐处理，形成标准化维度分数，再聚合为单一综合评分，支持不同架构和计算配置的模型在受控比较设置下进行直接对比。

Result: 在医疗、金融和社会经济数据集上的实验显示，高预测性能并不等同于更好的整体完整性与责任性；在某些情况下，简单模型比复杂深度表格架构表现出更强的跨维度平衡性。

Conclusion: MIRAI为受监管环境下的负责任模型选择提供了一个紧凑且实用的评估基础，强调了模型评估应超越预测性能，全面考虑多维度责任指标。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence in high-stakes tabular domains cannot be evaluated by predictive performance alone, yet current practice still assesses explainability, fairness, robustness, privacy, and sustainability mostly in isolation. We propose the Model Integrity and Responsibility Assessment Index (MIRAI), a unified evaluation framework that measures tabular models across these five dimensions under a controlled comparison setting and aggregates them into a single score. MIRAI combines established metrics through normalized and direction-aligned dimension scores, which enables direct comparison across models with different architectural and computational profiles. Experiments on healthcare, financial, and socioeconomic datasets show that higher predictive performance does not necessarily imply better overall integrity and responsibility. In several cases, simpler models achieve a stronger cross-dimensional balance than more complex deep tabular architectures. MIRAI provides a compact and practical basis for responsible model selection in regulated settings.

</details>


### [68] [SeesawNet: Towards Non-stationary Time Series Forecasting with Balanced Modeling of Common and Specific Dependencies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14551)
*Hao Li,Lu Zhang,Liu Chong,Yankai Chen,Pengyang Wang,Yingjie Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对实例归一化在非平稳多元时间序列预测中过度平滑实例特定结构信息的问题，提出SeesawNet统一架构，通过自适应平稳-非平稳注意力(ASNA)机制，根据实例的非平稳性动态融合归一化序列的共性依赖和原始序列的特定依赖，并在时间和通道维度交替建模，在多个基准上超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实例归一化虽能减少分布偏移并突出共性模式，但会过度平滑对建模时间和跨通道异质性至关重要的实例特定结构信息。先前方法要么继续压制分布差异，要么尝试恢复时间特定依赖，但都忽视了根据每个实例的非平稳结构自适应地建模共性和实例特定依赖这一核心矛盾。

Method: 提出SeesawNet统一架构，核心是自适应平稳-非平稳注意力(ASNA)，从归一化序列捕获共性依赖、从原始序列捕获特定依赖，并根据实例级非平稳性自适应融合；在此基础上交替进行时间和通道关系建模，联合捕获长程依赖和跨变量依赖。

Result: 在多个真实世界基准数据集上，SeesawNet一致性地超越了现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: SeesawNet通过自适应平衡共性和实例特定依赖建模，有效解决了非平稳时序预测中归一化带来的信息损失问题，在时间和通道维度上均取得了更好的建模效果。

Abstract: Instance normalization (IN) is widely used in non-stationary multivariate time series forecasting to reduce distribution shifts and highlight common patterns across samples. However, IN can over-smooth instance-specific structural information that is essential for modeling temporal and cross-channel heterogeneity. While prior methods further suppress distribution discrepancies or attempt to recover temporal specific dependencies, they often ignore a central tension: how to adaptively model common and instance-specific dependency based on each instance's non-stationary structures. To address this dilemma, we propose SeesawNet, a unified architecture that dynamically balances common and instance-specific dependency modeling in both temporal and channel dimensions. At its core is Adaptive Stationary-Nonstationary Attention (ASNA), which captures common dependencies from normalized sequences and specific dependencies from raw sequences, and adaptively fuses them according to instance-level non-stationarity. Built upon ASNA, SeesawNet alternates dedicated temporal and channel relationship modeling to jointly capture long-range and cross-variable dependencies. Extensive experiments on multiple real-world benchmarks demonstrate that SeesawNet consistently outperforms state-of-the-art methods.

</details>


### [69] [Efficient Multi-objective Prompt Optimization via Pure-exploration Bandits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14553)
*Donghao Li,Chengshuai Shi,Weijuan Ou,Cong Shen,Jing Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了多目标提示选择问题，将提示选择建模为纯探索多目标赌博机问题，分别针对Pareto最优提示集恢复和最佳可行提示识别两个实际场景，提出了理论保证的算法，并在多个大语言模型上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有提示选择方法大多忽略了一个关键挑战：提示性能本质上是多方面的（multi-faceted），无法由单一指标全面衡量。为填补这一空白，作者研究多目标提示选择问题。

Method: 将问题建模到纯探索赌博机（pure-exploration bandits）框架中，适配了多目标赌博机中已有的高效算法，并引入了一种针对结构化赌博机中最佳可行臂识别的新设计，在线性情况下提供了识别误差的理论保证。

Result: 在多个大语言模型上的大量实验表明，基于赌博机的方法相比基线方法取得了显著改进。

Conclusion: 建立了一个原则性且高效的多目标提示优化框架，为提示工程提供了理论基础和实用方法。

Abstract: Prompt engineering has become central to eliciting the capabilities of large language models (LLMs). At its core lies prompt selection -- efficiently identifying the most effective prompts. However, most prior investigations overlook a key challenge: the inherently multi-faceted nature of prompt performance, which cannot be captured by a single metric. To fill this gap, we study the multi-objective prompt selection problem under two practical settings: Pareto prompt set recovery and best feasible prompt identification. Casting the problem into the pure-exploration bandits framework, we adapt provably efficient algorithms from multi-objective bandits and further introduce a novel design for best feasible arm identification in structured bandits, with theoretical guarantees on the identification error in the linear case. Extensive experiments across multiple LLMs show that the bandit-based approaches yield significant improvements over baselines, establishing a principled and efficient framework for multi-objective prompt optimization.

</details>


### [70] [Resolving Action Bottleneck: Agentic Reinforcement Learning Informed by Token-Level Energy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14558)
*Langzhou He,Junyou Zhu,Yue Zhou,Zhengyao Gu,Junhua Liu,Wei-Chieh Huang,Henry Peng Zou,David Wipf,Philip S. Yu,Qitian Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现当前策略梯度方法（PPO、GRPO）在token级训练信号分配上存在严重偏差：动作token虽占轨迹比例极小，却集中了大部分与奖励方差相关的训练信号（即"动作瓶颈"现象）。为此提出ActFocus方法，通过降低推理token梯度权重并基于能量分布增加高不确定性动作token的权重，在无额外计算开销下实现显著性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有PPO、GRPO等策略梯度方法对轨迹中所有token进行均匀信用分配，未能区分推理token与动作token在训练信号上的本质差异，导致训练信号被错误分配，限制了智能体强化学习的效率。

Method: 从能量基模型视角出发，量化token级训练信号与奖励方差的相关性，揭示动作token是训练信号的集中载体。提出ActFocus方法：(1) 对推理token进行梯度降权；(2) 基于能量分布对高不确定性动作token进行额外增权。

Result: 在四个环境和不同模型规模上，ActFocus相比PPO和GRPO分别取得最高65.2和63.7个百分点的最终步数提升，且无需额外运行时或内存开销。

Conclusion: 均匀信用分配是当前智能体强化学习方法的关键瓶颈，针对token角色差异进行差异化梯度分配是一种简单高效的改进策略。

Abstract: Agentic reinforcement learning trains large language models using multi-turn trajectories that interleave long reasoning traces with short environment-facing actions. Common policy-gradient methods, such as PPO and GRPO, treat each token in a trajectory equally, leading to uniform credit assignment. In this paper, we critically demonstrate that such uniform credit assignment largely misallocates token-level training signals. From an energy-based modeling perspective, we show that token-level training signals, quantified by their correlations with reward variance of different rollouts sampled from a given prompt, concentrate sharply on action tokens rather than reasoning tokens, even though action tokens account for only a small fraction of the trajectory. We refer to this phenomenon as the Action Bottleneck. Motivated by this observation, we propose an embarrassingly simple token reweighting approach, ActFocus, that downweights gradients on reasoning tokens, along with an additional energy-based redistribution mechanism that further increases the weights on action tokens with higher uncertainty. Across four environments and different model sizes, ActFocus consistently outperforms PPO and GRPO, yielding final-step gains of up to 65.2 and 63.7 percentage points, respectively, without any additional runtime or memory cost.

</details>


### [71] [Woodelf++: A Fast and Unified Partial Dependence Plot Algorithm for Decision Tree Ensembles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14578)
*Ron Wettenstein,Alexander Nadel,Udi Boker*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Woodelf++ 是一个统一且高效的计算决策树集成模型可解释性工具（PDP、Joint-PDP、Any-Order-PDIVs 及 Full PDP）的框架，基于伪布尔函数推导出统一度量，在 Any-Order-PDIVs 上实现指数级复杂度改进，GPU 加速下比 scikit-learn 快五个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的 PDP、Joint-PDP、PDIVs 等可解释性工具各自独立实现，缺乏统一高效的计算框架；尤其是 Any-Order-PDIVs 任务计算所有特征子集的交互值，现有方法计算成本极高，亟需一种统一的、指数级加速的解决方案。

Method: 在 Woodelf（高效 SHAP 计算算法）的基础上，通过推导伪布尔函数上的合适度量，统一形式化 PDP、Joint-PDP、Any-Order-PDIVs 的计算；引入 Full PDP 概念，利用模型分裂阈值忠实捕捉所有特征值上的模型行为；支持近似计算与 GPU 加速，纯 Python 实现。

Result: 在 40 万行数据集上，PDP 和 Joint-PDP 计算速度比现有最优方法快 6 倍，比 scikit-learn 快五个数量级；Any-Order-PDIVs 全部交互值可在 5 分钟内完成，而现有方法预估需要超过 100 万年。

Conclusion: Woodelf++ 提供了一个统一的框架来高效计算多种决策树集成可解释性工具，显著降低了计算复杂度（尤其对 Any-Order-PDIVs 实现指数级加速），具有实际可用性。

Abstract: Partial Dependence Plots (PDPs) visualize how changes in a single feature affect the average model prediction. They are widely used in practice to interpret decision tree ensembles and other machine learning models. Joint-PDPs extend this idea to pairs of features, revealing their combined effect. Partial Dependence Interaction Values (PDIVs) measure feature interactions. The Any-Order-PDIVs task computes these interactions for every feature subset across all rows of the dataset.
  We introduce Woodelf++, a unified and efficient approach for computing all these useful explainability tools on decision tree ensembles, building on Woodelf, an algorithm for efficient SHAP computation. By deriving suitable metrics over pseudo-Boolean functions, Woodelf++ can compute PDPs (exact and approximate), Joint-PDPs, and Any-Order-PDIVs in a unified framework. Our method delivers substantial complexity improvements over the state of the art, including an exponential gain for Any-Order-PDIVs. Additionally, we introduce and efficiently compute Full PDPs, which leverage the model's split thresholds to faithfully capture its behavior across all possible feature values.
  Woodelf++ is implemented in pure Python and supports GPU acceleration. On a dataset with 400,000 rows, Woodelf++ computes PDP and Joint-PDP up to 6x faster than the state of the art and up to five orders of magnitude faster than scikit-learn. For Any-Order-PDIVs, the gap is even larger: Woodelf++ computes all interaction values in 5 minutes, while the state of the art is estimated to require over 1,000,000 years.

</details>


### [72] [Silent Collapse in Recursive Learning Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14588)
*Zhipeng Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现了一种名为"静默崩溃"的现象：在递归学习（模型用自身旧版本生成的数据训练）中，模型内部分布（预测熵、表示多样性、尾部覆盖）会逐渐收缩，而传统指标（损失、困惑度、准确率）依然稳定甚至改善。研究识别出三个可检测的前兆信号，并提出了MTR框架（监控—信任—调节器）来预警和防止该现象，无需依赖原始真实数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准性能指标在递归学习中无法早期检测模型内部退化，往往在退化不可逆时才发现问题。需要能在退化发生前提供预警的信号和方法。

Method: 通过分析递归学习过程中模型内部分布的轨迹，识别出三个前兆信号：(1) 锚点熵收缩；(2) 表示漂移冻结；(3) 尾部覆盖侵蚀。基于这些信号提出MTR框架，包含监控轨迹统计、估计慢时间尺度信任变量、自适应调节有效学习强度。

Result: 三个前兆信号在标准验证指标退化之前的多个世代即可被检测到。MTR框架能够提供早期预警并主动防止静默崩溃，且无需访问原始真实数据，适用于原始数据不可用、污染或私有的场景。

Conclusion: 静默崩溃是递归学习中的系统性风险，但其发生并非突然，而是有可检测的轨迹级前兆。MTR作为一种轻量级元认知回路，可以有效预防这一问题，对大规模递归训练系统的安全性和可持续性具有重要意义。

Abstract: Recursive learning -- where models are trained on data generated by previous versions of themselves -- is increasingly common in large language models, autonomous agents, and self-supervised systems. However, standard performance metrics (loss, perplexity, accuracy) often fail to detect internal degradation before it becomes irreversible. Here we identify a phenomenon we call silent collapse: under broad recursive conditions, model internal distributions -- predictive entropy, representational diversity, and tail coverage -- progressively contract even as conventional metrics appear stable or improving.
  We discover that silent collapse is not abrupt. Its onset is reliably preceded by three trajectory-level precursors: (1) contraction of anchor entropy, (2) freezing of representation drift, and (3) erosion of tail coverage. These signals manifest multiple generations before any degradation in standard validation metrics, enabling early warning.
  Based on these precursors, we propose the MTR (Monitor--Trust--Regulator) framework, a lightweight metacognitive loop that monitors trajectory statistics, estimates a slow-timescale trust variable, and adaptively modulates the effective learning intensity. MTR provides early warning and actively prevents silent collapse without requiring access to pristine real data -- a critical advantage when original data is unavailable, contaminated, or private.

</details>


### [73] [Fast Rates for Inverse Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14599)
*Andreas Schlaginhaufen,Maryam Kamgarpour*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文建立了熵正则化Min-Max逆强化学习在线性奖励函数类、有限时域Borel状态-动作空间MDP中的结构与统计结果：在结构上证明了MLE与Min-Max-IRRL在总体层面以及确定性动力学下的经验层面等价；在统计上利用伪自协调性证明了轨迹级KL散度和Hessian范数下的平方参数误差均以O(n^{-1})的快速率衰减，且结果适用于模型误设且无需探索假设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为熵正则化Min-Max逆强化学习建立严格的结构等价性（MLE与Min-Max-IRL）和快速统计收敛率，弥补现有理论在一般Borel空间和模型误设下的空白。

Method: 利用伪自协调性（pseudo-self-concordance）分析Min-Max-IRL损失函数；在线性奖励函数类框架下进行结构等价性证明；在有限时域MDP（Borel状态-动作空间）中推导统计收敛率。

Result: 1) 在总体水平上MLE与Min-Max-IRL等价；在确定性动力学下经验水平等价。2) 轨迹级KL散度以O(n^{-1})速率衰减。3) Hessian范数下的平方参数误差以O(n^{-1})速率衰减。4) 结果适用于模型误设且无需探索假设。5) 将奖励可识别性结果推广至一般Borel空间。6) 得到了软最优值函数关于奖励参数的导数新结果。

Conclusion: 本文为熵正则化Min-Max-IRL提供了完整的结构等价性理论和快速统计收敛保证，显著拓展了逆强化学习的理论基础，特别是在一般Borel空间和模型误设条件下的适用性。

Abstract: We establish novel structural and statistical results for entropy-regularized min-max inverse reinforcement learning (Min-Max-IRL) with linear reward classes in finite-horizon MDPs with Borel state and action spaces. On the structural side, we show that maximum likelihood estimation (MLE) and Min-Max-IRL are equivalent at the population level, and at the empirical level under deterministic dynamics. On the statistical side, exploiting pseudo-self-concordance of the Min-Max-IRL loss, we prove that both the trajectory-level KL divergence and the squared parameter error in the Hessian norm decay at the fast rate $\mathcal{O}(n^{-1})$, where $n$ is the number of expert trajectories. Our guarantees apply under misspecification and require no exploration assumptions. We further extend reward-identifiability results to general Borel spaces and derive novel results on the derivatives of the soft-optimal value function with respect to reward parameters.

</details>


### [74] [Unbiased and Second-Order-Free Training for High-Dimensional PDEs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14643)
*Jaemin Seo,Surin Lee,Jae Yong Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文分析了基于欧拉-丸山（EM）时间离散化在BSDE方法求解高维PDE时引入的偏差，并提出了一种无偏且无需二阶导数的训练框架，在保持计算优势的同时消除了偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: BSDE方法在求解高维PDE时表现出色，但常用的EM时间离散化会引入固有偏差；高阶格式虽能消除偏差却重新引入二阶空间导数并带来巨大计算开销。因此，需要一种既能消除偏差又能保持计算效率的无偏训练框架。

Method: 对EM时间离散化引入的损失偏差进行原理性分析，并基于此提出一种无偏、无需二阶导数的训练框架，避免高阶格式（如Heun）带来的二阶导数计算负担。

Result: 提出了一种无偏且无需二阶导数的BSDE训练框架，在消除EM偏差的同时保持计算优势。

Conclusion: 该工作为BSDE方法提供了偏差校正的理论基础，并实现了高效的无偏训练方案，代码已开源。

Abstract: Deep learning methods based on backward stochastic differential equations (BSDEs) have emerged as competitive alternatives to physics-informed neural networks (PINNs) for solving high-dimensional partial differential equations (PDEs). By leveraging probabilistic representations, BSDE approaches can avoid the curse of dimensionality and often admit second-order-free training objectives that do not require explicit Hessian evaluations. It has recently been established that the commonly used Euler-Maruyama (EM) time discretization induces an intrinsic bias in BSDE training losses. While high-order schemes such as Heun can fully eliminate this bias, such schemes re-introduce second-order spatial derivatives and incur substantial computational overhead. In this work, we provide a principled analysis of EM-induced loss bias and propose an unbiased, second-order-free training framework that preserves the computational advantages of BSDE methods. Our code is available at https://github.com/seojaemin22/Un-EM-BSDE.

</details>


### [75] [Slower Generalization, Faster Memorization: A Sweet Spot in Algorithmic Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14659)
*Shin So,Kyelim Lee,Albert No*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在结构化输出任务（Needleman-Wunsch矩阵生成）中，小型Transformer存在一个数据集大小的"甜点"：中等规模的数据集能使验证准确率最快收敛，而更大的数据集反而需要更多梯度更新才能泛化。这挑战了"数据越多越好"的直觉，表明规则学习的临界数据量与优化更新收敛的数据量并不一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 挑战"临界数据量"理论中关于数据越多验证收敛越快的自然直觉，揭示在结构化输出任务中该直觉可能失效。

Method: 在Needleman-Wunsch（NW）矩阵生成任务上训练小型Transformer，通过变化数据集大小观察验证准确率的收敛行为，并与乘法基线任务进行对比。

Result: 1) NW任务中存在数据集大小的"甜点"，中等规模数据使验证准确率最快收敛；2) 超过甜点后，泛化仍可实现但需更多梯度更新；3) 在部分验证能力首次出现的阶段，更大数据集反而能加速训练准确率提升；4) 乘法基线任务未表现出相同的过阈减速现象。

Conclusion: 规则学习的临界数据量与优化更新收敛的最优数据量是分离的，结构化输出任务中学习规则与完成精确拟合的路径可能不同。

Abstract: Critical-data-size accounts of grokking suggest a natural post-threshold intuition: once training data is sufficient to identify the underlying rule, additional data should accelerate validation convergence. We show that this intuition can fail in a controlled structured-output task.
